\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\left[{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}^{2}-{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)}^{2}\right]\Bigg\},

(61)

where in
(a)
we neglected
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)-\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}(t)
with similar arguments as before, and neglected term
(b)
as it is small compared to
\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)
as we assume
r_{1},...,r_{d}
are large.

With this conditional-Gaussian approximation, (
54
) becomes

\displaystyle~p_{\mathbf{x}\rightarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}(t,\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\log\int_{u_{\mathbf{x}}}p\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\times\mathcal{N}\left(\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}};\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})={\mathcal{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right\},\operatorname{Var}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})={\mathcal{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right\}\right)+\text{const}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~H_{\mathbf{x}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})={\mathcal{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right\},\operatorname{Var}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})={\mathcal{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right\};v_{\mathbf{x}}\right)+\text{const}

(62)

in terms of the function

\displaystyle H_{\mathbf{x}}\left(\hat{p},\nu^{p};v\right)\triangleq\log\int_{u}p(v|u)\mathcal{N}\left(u;\hat{p},\nu^{p}\right).

(63)

Rewriting (
62
) using a Taylor series expansion in
{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}
about the point
{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)}
, using (
58
) and (
61
) we obtain

\displaystyle p_{\mathbf{x}\rightarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}(t,\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i}))

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle~\text{const}+H_{\mathbf{x}}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})

\displaystyle+\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\left[\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})-\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\right]\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}(t)\right]

\displaystyle~\times H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\left[\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})-\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\right]\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}(t)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle~\times H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime\prime}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})

\displaystyle+\dot{H}_{\mathbf{x}}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})\times\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\left[{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}^{2}-{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)}^{2}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle~\text{const}+

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\left[\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})-\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\right]\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\left[\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})-\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\right]\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}(t)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle~\times\left(-\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)}\right]\times\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\left[{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}^{2}-{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)}^{2}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}(t)\right]\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}(t)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle~\times\left[\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})^{2}-2\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right]\left(-\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)}\right]\left[{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}^{2}\right]\times

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}(t)\right]\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle~\times\left[\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})^{2}-2\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right]\left(-\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)}\right]\left[{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}^{2}\right]\times

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg],

(64)

where
(a)
follows from putting some constants without
\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})
in “const” and from below definitions

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})

(65)

\displaystyle\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq-H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime\prime}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})

(66)

together with the fact that according to
(
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014a
)
the following general relationship holds

\displaystyle{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\triangleq\dot{H}_{\mathbf{x}}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})=\frac{1}{2}\left[H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})^{2}+H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime\prime}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})\right]=\frac{1}{2}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)}\right],

(67)

and
(b)
follows from putting some constants without
\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})
in “const”. Moreover,
(c)
follows as we replace
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)
and
{\nu}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)
by
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)
and
{\nu}_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)
, respectively, since recall that we assumed that data size is large and
p_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}(t,\cdot)
and
p_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}(t,\cdot)
differ by only one term (which vanishes relative to the others in the large-system limit). See
(
Rangan 2011
;
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014a
;
Montanari, Eldar, and Kutyniok 2012
)
for similar arguments.

It is argued in
(
Rangan 2011
;
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014a
)
that given (
65
)-(
66
) and (
63
) the followings hold

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)}\left(\hat{u}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\right)

(68)

\displaystyle{\nu}_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)}\left(1-\frac{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)}\right),

(69)

for the conditional mean and variance

\displaystyle\hat{u}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{p}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)\right\}

(70)

\displaystyle\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{p}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)\right\},

(71)

computed according to the conditional pdf

\displaystyle p_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{p}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(u|\hat{p};\nu^{p}\right)\triangleq\frac{p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\mathcal{N}(u_{\mathbf{x}};\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t))}{\int_{u^{\prime}}p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u^{\prime}\right)\mathcal{N}(u^{\prime};\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t))}.

(72)

In fact, (
72
) is TeG-AMP’s the
t
-th iteration approximation to the true marginal posterior
p_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{V}}(\cdot|\mathcal{V})
. We note that (
72
) can also be interpreted as the posterior pdf for
\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}
given the likelihood
p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|\cdot\right)
from (
3
) and the prior
\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}\sim\mathcal{N}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t))
that is implicitly assumed by the
t
-th iteration TeG-AMP.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle~p_{\mathbf{x}\rightarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\left(t,\mathcal{Z}_{i}(\ell_{i},x_{i},\ell_{i+1})\right)

\displaystyle{\approx}

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}(t)\right]\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle~\times\left[\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})^{2}-2\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right]\left(-\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)}\right]\left[{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}^{2}\right]\times

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~{\text{const}}+\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,\mathbf{x}}(t)}\Big]\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)+\frac{1}{2}{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}}(t){\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}^{2}{\zeta}_{x}(t)-\frac{1}{2}\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x}(t)}\Big]^{2}\left[\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})^{2}-2\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right]\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t),

where
{{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)}\triangleq{{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)}
,

{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)}\triangleq{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)}
,
\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell}\triangleq\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}
,

\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}\triangleq\mathbf{r}\backslash(r_{i},r_{i+1})
,
\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}\triangleq\mathbf{l}^{\prime}\backslash(\ell_{i}^{\prime},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime})
and

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,{\mathbf{x}}}(t)}

\displaystyle=\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}(t),

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x}(t)}

\displaystyle=\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t),

\displaystyle\zeta_{x}(t)

\displaystyle={\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\mathbb{D}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{{{\mathcal{Z}}},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\times\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{D}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg].

Then we complete the derivation.

Appendix C
Derivation of Equation (
20
)

Element-wise Result:

p_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\left(t+1,\mathcal{Z}_{i}(\ell_{i},x_{i},\ell_{i+1})\right)
in (
9
) can be approximated by

\displaystyle p_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\left(t+1,\mathcal{Z}_{i}(\ell_{i},x_{i},\ell_{i+1})\right)\ {\approx}\ \text{const}\ +\log\Big(p\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)\mathcal{N}\left({\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})};\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}\right)\Big),

where
{{\mathcal{Z}}_{i}^{\ell}}\triangleq{{\mathcal{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}}
and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}},\\
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)-{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t){\zeta}_{x}(t)\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}},\\
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1+\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}},\\
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t){\zeta}_{x}(t)\Bigg\}.

Derivation:

Recall (
9
) as

\displaystyle p_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow x_{i}}(\mathcal{Z}_{i}(:,x_{i},:))=

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\log p\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\right)+\sum_{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}p_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\rightarrow x_{i}},

which can be expanded as the following element-wise version

\displaystyle p_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}(t+1,\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i}))=\log p\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right)+\sum_{{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}}p_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\rightarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}(t,\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i}))+\text{const}.

(73)

Starting with (
73
) and plugging in (
16
) we obtain

\displaystyle~p_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}(t+1,\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\log p\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right)+\sum_{{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}}p_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\rightarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}(t,\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i}))+\text{const}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\log p\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right)+\sum_{{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle~\times\left[\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})^{2}-2\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right]\left(-\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\right)+\frac{1}{2}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\left[{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}^{2}\right]\times

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\log p\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right)+{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}^{2}\sum_{{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\left(-\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\right)+\frac{1}{2}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\times

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\sum_{{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}}\Bigg\{\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle-\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\left(-\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\log p\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right)-\frac{1}{2\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}}\left({\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})}-\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\log\left(p\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})\right)\mathcal{N}\left({\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})};\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right)\right),

(74)

where
(a)
follows from (
16
),
(b)
follows from the following definitions and
{\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}^{2}}/{2\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}}
is a constant without
\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(x_{i})
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}}\triangleq

\displaystyle~\sum_{{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}}\Bigg\{\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle-\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\times

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\},

(75)

and

\displaystyle~~~~~\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}

\displaystyle\triangleq\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}}\Bigg\{\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~-\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\left(-\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}}\Bigg\{\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle~~~+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1+\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\}}}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\times

\displaystyle~~~~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}.

(76)

Denote
\mathbf{q}^{i}\triangleq[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]
, we have

\displaystyle p_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\left(t+1,\mathcal{Z}_{i}(\ell_{i},x_{i},\ell_{i+1})\right)\ {\approx}\ \text{const}\ +\log\Big(p\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)\mathcal{N}\left({\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})};\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}\right)\Big),

where
{{\mathcal{Z}}_{i}^{\ell}}\triangleq{{\mathcal{Z}}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}}
and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}},\\
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)-{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t){\zeta}_{x}(t)\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}},\\
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1+\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}},\\
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t){\zeta}_{x}(t)\Bigg\}.

Then we complete the derivation.

Appendix D
Derivation of Equation (
23
)

Element-wise Result:

\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t+1)
can be approximated by

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t+1)=g\left(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}\right)\approx\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t+1)-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{{{\mathcal{Z}}},\ell}(t+1)\times{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x}(t)},

where
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}\triangleq g(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}})
and
\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{{{\mathcal{Z}}},\ell}\triangleq\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}g^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}})
with

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)-{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t){\zeta}_{x}(t)\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1+\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t){\zeta}_{x}(t)\Bigg\}.

Derivation:

Recall that the mean and variance of the pdf associated with the
p_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}(t+1,\cdot)
approximation in (
20
) can be expanded by

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t+1)\triangleq\underbrace{\frac{1}{C}\int_{x}x~p(x)\mathcal{N}\left(x;\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right)}_{\triangleq~g(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]})},

(77)

and

\displaystyle\nu_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t+1)\triangleq\underbrace{\frac{1}{C}\int_{x}|x-\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t+1)|^{2}~p(x)\mathcal{N}\left(x;\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right)}_{\triangleq~\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}g^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]})},

(78)

where here
C=\int_{x}p(x)\mathcal{N}\left(x;\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right)
and
g^{\prime}
denotes the derivative of
g
with respect to the first argument. The fact that (
77
) and (
78
) are related through a derivative was shown in
(
Rangan 2011
)
.

We now derive approximations of
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}
and
\nu_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}
that avoid the dependence on the destination node
u_{\mathbf{x}}
. For this, we introduce
\mathbf{x}
-invariant versions of
\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}
and
\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}
as follows

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}}\triangleq

\displaystyle~\sum_{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}\Bigg\{\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle-\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\times

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\},

(79)

and

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}

\displaystyle\triangleq\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}\Bigg\{\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle~~~+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1+\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\times

\displaystyle~~~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}.

(80)

Remark D.1
.

Comparing (
79
)-(
80
) with (
75
)-(
76
) suggest that
\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}-\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}
is relatively negligible and hence each
\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}
can be approximated by the term

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}-\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\times\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\right]\times\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),

(81)

Hence, (
77
) implies the following approximation, we have

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t+1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~g\left(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~g\Bigg(\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}-\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}

\displaystyle\times\Big[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\Bigg)

(82)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~g\left(\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right)-g^{\prime}\left(\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right)

\displaystyle\times\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\Big[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle~\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t+1)-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t+1)

\displaystyle\times\Big[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big].

(83)

Above,
(a)
follows from Remark
D.1
;
(b)
follows from a Taylor series expansion in the first argument of (
82
) about the point
\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}
; and
(c)
follows by applying the definitions

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t+1)\triangleq g\left(\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right),

(84)

and

\displaystyle\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t+1)\triangleq\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}g^{\prime}\left(\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\right),

(85)

which match (
77
)-(
78
) without the
\mathbf{x}
-dependence.

Remark D.2
.

Likewise, taking Taylor series expansions of
g^{\prime}
in (
78
) about the point
\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}
in the first argument and about the point
\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}
in the second argument and then comparing the result with (
85
) confirms that
\nu_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)-\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)
is negligible.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t+1)=g\left(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{q}^{i}}\right)\approx\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t+1)-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{{{\mathcal{Z}}},\ell}(t+1)\times{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x}(t)},

where
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}\triangleq g(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}})
and
\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{{{\mathcal{Z}}},\ell}\triangleq\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}g^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}})
with

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)-{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t){\zeta}_{x}(t)\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1+\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}{\omega}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t){\zeta}_{x}(t)\Bigg\}.

Then we complete the derivation.

E.1
Derivation of Equation (

Element-wise Result:

\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
in (

) can be approximated by

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\approx\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),

where

and

\displaystyle\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\mathbb{D}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big(\prod_{i\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Big)^{2}\times\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{D}\setminus A}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Big(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\prod_{i^{\prime}\in\mathbb{D}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}(t)\Big)^{d-|A|-1}\Bigg\}.

Derivation:

Plug (
23
) into (

) to obtain

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\approx}\underbrace{\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}(t)}}_{\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)}+\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t-1)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t-1)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\approx}\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)+\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t-1)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t-1)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\approx}\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)+\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\approx}\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),

(86)

where
(a)
follows from (
11
);
(b)
follows from (

);

follows noting that we assumed data size is large and equations on two side of this differ by only one term (which vanishes relative to the others in the large-system limit); for

\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}(t)

and the following definition:

\displaystyle\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big(\prod_{i\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i}^{\prime},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\Big)^{2}\times

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Big(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\prod_{i^{\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}(t)\Big)^{d-|A|-1}\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}.

(87)

Remark E.1
.

We observed that the following term is small

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t).

(88)

Hence, we have

\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
in (
11
) can be approximated by

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\approx\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),

where
\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\triangleq\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,x}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,x}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}
and

\displaystyle\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\mathbb{D}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big(\prod_{i\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Big)^{2}\times\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{D}\setminus A}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Big(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\prod_{i^{\prime}\in\mathbb{D}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}(t)\Big)^{d-|A|-1}\Bigg\}.

Then we complete the derivation. Note that we neglected small terms in Remark
E.1
, we observed that the approximations work well. If under specific applications this term was not negligible, it can simply be included back in the algorithm.

Element-wise Result:

{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\Bigg[\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)-

\displaystyle~~~\times\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\approx}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1},...,\ell_{d}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\prod_{i\in\{1,...,d\}}\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\\

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\approx}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1},...,\ell_{d}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\prod_{i\in\{1,...,d\}}\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}(t)+\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),

where
(a)
follows from (

), for

we used
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}(t)
in place of
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}(t-1)
and

E.2

Remark E.2
.

We observed that the following approximations hold

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\Bigg[\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)-

\displaystyle~~~\Big[\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in A}\Bigg[\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)-

\displaystyle~~~\Big[\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in A}\Bigg[\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)-

\displaystyle~~~2\Big[\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\approx}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)}\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\},

(90)

where in
(a)
we neglect the term

\displaystyle\Big[\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\Big]^{2}

with similar arguments as before, and
(b)
follows from that we observed the following approximation holds:

\displaystyle\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in A}\Bigg[\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)-2\Big[\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]

\displaystyle~~~\times\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)}\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\Big[-\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\}.

(91)

We observed that the above approximation holds well since the difference between the left-hand side and the right-hand side is small, note that if under specific applications the above approximation was not applicable, it can simply be included back in the algorithm.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}^{\prime}=\mathbf{1}\end{subarray}}^{\mathbf{r}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\mathbb{D}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\left(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{{{\mathcal{Z}}},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{D}\setminus A}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\right)\approx\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}=\mathbf{1}\end{subarray}}^{\mathbf{r}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{D}}\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{{{\mathcal{Z}}},\ell}(t)+\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t).

Then we complete the derivation.

E.3
Derivation of Equation (
27
)

Element-wise Result:

\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}
and
\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}
in (

) can be respectively approximated by

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}}\approx\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\Bigg\},

and

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}~{\approx}~\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\Bigg[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\Bigg]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\left\{1-\frac{\sum\limits_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}{\zeta}_{x}(t)}{\sum\limits_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\left[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\right]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\Bigg\}}\right\}.

) into

\displaystyle~~~~~~\hat{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}

\displaystyle~~~+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1-\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\times

\displaystyle~~~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\

\setminus\{i\},A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\

(92)

where
(a)
follows from neglecting the below term with the same arguments as before.

\displaystyle\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle-\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\Bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\right]\times

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\

\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(x)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~-\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\Bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle~\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\left[{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\right]\times

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\},

(93)

where
(x)
follows Remark (

). We observed that the term (

) is small, if under specific applications this term was not negligible, it can simply be included back in the algorithm.

And for
(b)
, we had the approximation:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}}\approx\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\Bigg\},

(94)

by neglecting
{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-\nu^{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)
terms as explained in Appendix B in
(
Parker, Schniter, and

)
.

Remark E.3
.

The following holds:

\displaystyle\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(z)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\nu^{r}_{[i,\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1},x_{i}]}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell_{1}^{\prime},...,\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\Bigg[{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}(t)}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}(t)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
