A\neq\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\}\Bigg]\Bigg\},

(95)

where
(z)
follows from

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}(t)}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}(t)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t-1)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t-1)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}^{\ell_{i-1}^{\prime},\ell_{i}}(t)}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}^{\ell_{i+1},\ell_{i+2}^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime}}(t)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A,\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i}\\
\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\\
\ell_{i}^{\prime}=\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}^{\prime}=\ell_{i+1}\end{subarray}}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\sum_{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime},...,\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime}=1}^{r_{1},...,r_{i-1},r_{i+2},...,r_{d}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+2}^{\prime\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{d}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{1}^{\prime\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\},

(96)

where
(a)
follows from (
23
) and in
(b)
we used
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}(t)
in place of
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime\prime},\ell_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}(t-1)
.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}}\approx\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\Big[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\Big]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\Bigg\},

and

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}~{\approx}~\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}\sum_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\Bigg[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\Bigg]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}+\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\left\{1-\frac{\sum\limits_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}{\zeta}_{x}(t)}{\sum\limits_{{{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}}}\Bigg\{\left[{\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}_{\setminus i}=1}^{\mathbf{r}_{\setminus i}}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{\setminus i,x^{\prime}}(t)}\right]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\Bigg\}}\right\}.

Then we complete the derivation.

Appendix F
Algorithm Summary and Complexity Analysis

The TeG-AMP algorithm derived is summarized in Algorithm
1
. There, we have included a maximum number of iterations
T_{\text{max}}
, and a stopping condition in Algorithm
1
based on the change in the residual and a user-defined parameter
\tau_{\text{threshold}}
. We have also written the algorithm in a general form that allows the use of complex-valued quantities, in which case
\mathcal{N}
in (
15
) and (
30
) would be circular complex Gaussian. For ease of interpretation, Algorithm
1
does not include the important damping modifications that will be detailed in Appendix
E
. Suggestions for the initializations in Algorithm
1
will be given in the following. Note that TeG-AMP avoids the use of SVD or QR decompositions, lending itself to simple and potentially parallel implementations.

Algorithm 1

Tensor Generalized Approximate Message Passing (TeG-AMP) Algorithm.

Initialization:

p_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{p}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(u|\hat{p};\nu^{p}\right)=\frac{p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u\right)\mathcal{N}(u;\hat{p}(t),\nu^{p}(t))}{\int_{u^{\prime}}p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u^{\prime}\right)\mathcal{N}(u^{\prime};\hat{p}(t),\nu^{p}(t))}
;

p_{\mathsf{Z}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)|\mathsf{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}}\left(z|\hat{r};\nu^{r}\right)=\frac{p\left(z\right)\mathcal{N}\left(z;\hat{r},\nu^{r}\right)}{\int_{z^{\prime}}p\left(z^{\prime}\right)\mathcal{N}\left(z^{\prime};\hat{r},\nu^{r}\right)}
;

\forall i,x_{i},\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(0)=0
;

\forall i,\ell_{i},x_{i}
,
choose

\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(1)
,
\nu^{\mathcal{Z},\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(1)
.

1:
for

t=1,\cdots,T_{\text{max}}

do

2:
\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\leftarrow\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}
[according to (
24
)]

3: Update
\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
[according to (
25
)]

4: Update
\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
[according to (
24
)]

5: Update
\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
[according to (
26
)]

6:
\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)\leftarrow\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{p}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)\right\}
[according to (
14
)]

7:
\hat{u}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\leftarrow\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{p}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)\right\}
[according to (
13
)]

8:
\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\leftarrow\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)}\left(\hat{u}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\right)
[according to (
17
)]

9:
{\nu}_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)\leftarrow\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)}\left(1-\frac{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)}\right)
[according to (
18
)]

10: Update
\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}
[according to (
27
)]

11: Update
\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}
[according to (
28
)]

12:
\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{{\mathcal{Z}},\ell}(t+1)\leftarrow\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathsf{Z}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}|\mathsf{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}\leftarrow\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}};\nu_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}^{r}\right\}
[according to (
30
)]

13:
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t+1)\leftarrow\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{Z}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}|\mathsf{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}=\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}};\nu_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}^{r}\right\}
[according to (
30
)]

14:
If

\sum_{\mathbf{x}}|\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)|^{2}\leq\tau_{\text{threshold}}\sum_{\mathbf{x}}|\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)|^{2}

then

15:
stop

16:
end for

The steps in Algorithm
1
can be interpreted as follows. Steps 2-3 compute a
plug-in
estimate
\bar{\mathcal{P}}
of the tensor
{\mathcal{U}}
and a corresponding set of element-wise variances
\{\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}\}
. Steps 4-5 can be seen as a correction (see
(
Rangan 2011
;
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014a
;
Montanari, Eldar, and Kutyniok 2012
)
for discussions in the contexts of BiG-AMP, G-AMP and
AMP, respectively) to obtain the corresponding quantities
\hat{\mathcal{P}}
and
\{\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}\}
. Using these quantities, steps 6-7 compute the approximate marginal posterior means
\hat{\mathcal{U}}
and variances of
\{\nu^{u}_{\mathbf{x}}\}
.
Steps 8-9 then use these posterior moments to compute the scaled residual
\hat{\mathcal{S}}
and a set of residual-variances
\{\nu^{s}_{\mathbf{x}}\}
.

Steps 10-11 then use the residual terms
\hat{\mathcal{S}}
and
\{\nu^{s}_{\mathbf{x}}\}
to compute
\hat{\mathcal{R}}
and
\{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}\}
, where
\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}
can be interpreted as a
\{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}\}
-variance-AWGN corrupted observation of the true
\mathsf{Z}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}_{i}(x_{i})
. Finally, steps 12-13 merge these AWGN-corrupted observations with the priors
p_{\mathsf{Z}^{\ell}_{i}(x_{i})}
to produce the posterior means
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}
and variances
\nu^{\mathcal{Z},\ell}_{i,x_{i}}
.

The complexity order is dominated by (
25
) [in steps 3], as the traverse addition and multiplication. So, the complexity of Algorithm
1
is
O(d^{4}\prod_{i=1}^{d}{r_{i}})
.
r_{i}
is generally small for low-rank problems. Therefore, the complexity is still acceptable.

Appendix G
Adaptive Damping

The approximations, e.g., 1) central limit theorem; 2) Taylor series argument; and 3) ignoring some terms, made in the TeG-AMP derivation were obtained in the large system limit. In practical applications, however, these dimensions are finite, and hence the algorithm may diverge. The use of damping with G-AMP and BiG-AMP achieves convergence with arbitrary matrices
(
Rangan 2011
;
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014a
;
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014b
)
. Here, we introduce to incorporate damping into TeG-AMP. Moreover, we adapt the damping of these variables to ensure that a particular cost criterion decreases monotonically, as described in the sequel. The specific damping strategy that we adopt is similar to those described in
(
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014a
;
Schniter and Rangan 2014
)
.

G.1
Damping

In TeG-AMP, the
t
-th iteration damping factor
\beta(t)\in(0,1]
is used to slow the evolution of certain variables, namely
\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}
,
\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}
,
\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}
,
\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}
and
\hat{z}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}
. To do this, steps 3, 5, 8, and 9 in Algorithm
1
are replaced with

\displaystyle\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle=(1-\beta(t))\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)

\displaystyle~~~~~+\beta(t)\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\mathbb{D}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big(\prod_{i\in A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Big)^{2}\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{D}\setminus A}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Big(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\prod_{i^{\prime}\in\mathbb{D}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}(t)\Big)^{d-|A|-1}\Bigg\},

(97)

\displaystyle\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta(t)\left(\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}=1\end{subarray}}^{\mathbf{r}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{D}}\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell}(t)\right)+(1-\beta(t))\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1),

(98)

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta(t)\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)}\left(\hat{u}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\right)+(1-\beta(t))\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1),

(99)

\displaystyle{\nu}_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta(t)\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)}\left(1-\frac{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}(t)}\right)+(1-\beta(t)){\nu}_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t-1),

(100)

and the following are inserted between step 9 and step 10 in Algorithm
1
:

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta(t){\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t)+(1-\beta(t)){\bar{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t-1).

(101)

The newly defined state variables
{\bar{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t)
are then used in place of
{\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t)
in steps 10 and 11 of Algorithm
1
. When
\beta(t)=1
, the damping has no effect, whereas when
\beta(t)=0
, all quantities become frozen in
t
.

G.2
Adaptive Damping

The idea behind adaptive damping is to monitor a chosen cost criterion
J(t)
and decrease
\beta(t)
when the cost has not decreased sufficiently relative to
\{J(\tau)\}_{\tau=t-1-T}^{t-1}
for some step window
T\geq 0
. This mechanism allows the cost criterion to increase over short intervals of
T
iterations and in this sense is similar to the procedure used in
(
Wright, Nowak, and Figueiredo 2009
)
. We now describe how the cost criterion
J(t)
is constructed, building on ideas in
(
Rangan et al. 2016
)
. The same approach is used in the BiG-AMP in
(
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014a
;
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014b
)
.

For fixed observations
\mathcal{V}
, the joint posterior pdf solves the KL-divergence minimization problem

\displaystyle p_{\mathsf{Z}|\mathsf{V}}=\arg\min_{b_{\mathsf{Z}}}D\left(b_{\mathsf{Z}}\|p_{\mathsf{Z}|\mathsf{V}}\right).

(102)

The factorized form (
5
) of the posterior allows writing

\displaystyle D\left(b_{\mathsf{Z}}\|p_{\mathsf{Z}|\mathsf{V}}\right)-p_{\mathsf{V}}(\mathcal{V})=

\displaystyle~\int_{\mathsf{Z}}b_{\mathsf{Z}}(\mathcal{A})\log\frac{b_{\mathsf{Z}}(\mathcal{Z})}{p_{\mathsf{V}|\mathsf{U}}(\mathcal{V}|\mathcal{U})p_{\mathsf{Z}}(\mathcal{Z})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~D\left(b_{\mathsf{Z}}\|p_{\mathsf{Z}}\right)-\int_{\mathsf{Z}}b_{\mathsf{Z}}(\mathcal{Z})\log p_{\mathsf{V}|\mathsf{U}}(\mathcal{V}|\mathcal{U}).

(103)

Equations (
102
) and (
103
) then imply that

\displaystyle p_{\mathsf{Z}|\mathsf{V}}

\displaystyle=\arg\min_{b_{\mathsf{Z}}}J\left(b_{\mathsf{Z}}\right),

(104)

\displaystyle J\left(b_{\mathsf{Z}}\right)

\displaystyle\triangleq D\left(b_{\mathsf{Z}}\|p_{\mathsf{Z}}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{Z}}}\left\{\log p_{\mathsf{V}|\mathsf{U}}(\mathcal{V}|\mathcal{U})\right\}.

(105)

To decide whether a given time-
t
TeG-AMP approximation
b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t)
of the joint posterior
p_{\mathsf{Z}|\mathsf{V}}
is preferred than the previous approximation
b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t-1)
, one could essentially plug the posterior approximation expressions (
15
) into (
104
) and then check whether
J(b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t))<J(b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t-1))
. But, since the expectation in (
104
) is difficult to evaluate, we approximate the cost (
104
) by using, in place of
\mathcal{U}
, an independent Gaussian tensor whose component means and variances are matched to those of
\mathcal{U}
. Taking the joint TeG-AMP posterior approximation
b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t)
to be the product of the marginals from (
15
), the resulting component means and variances are

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{l}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t)}\left\{\mathsf{Z}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell}\right\}\cdots\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t)}\left\{\mathsf{Z}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell}\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{l}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell}(t)=\bar{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}_{b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime}=1}^{\mathbf{r}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\mathbb{D}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{D}\backslash A}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)^{2}\Bigg).

(106)

To simplify the expressions, we define

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{b}\triangleq\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\};\mathrm{Var}_{b_{\mathsf{Z}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\}\right).

(107)

The approximate
t
-th iteration cost becomes

\displaystyle\hat{J}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq d\\
1\leq\ell_{i}\leq r_{i}\\
1\leq x_{i}\leq n_{i}\end{subarray}}D\Big(p_{\mathsf{Z}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}|r_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}}\big[\cdot|\hat{r}_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}(t);\nu_{\mathbf{q}^{i}}^{r}(t)\big]\|p_{\mathsf{Z}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}}(\cdot)\Big)-\sum_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}\sim\mathcal{N}_{b}}{\left\{\log p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\right\}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq d\\
1\leq\ell_{i}\leq r_{i}\\
1\leq x_{i}\leq n_{i}\end{subarray}}\int_{\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right);\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t),\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell}(t)\right)\times\log\frac{\mathcal{N}\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right);\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t),\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathcal{Z},\ell}(t)\right)}{\mathcal{N}\left(\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right);\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{\text{prior}},\nu_{\text{prior}}^{\mathcal{Z}}\right)}

\displaystyle~~~~-\sum_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}\sim\mathcal{N}_{b}}{\left\{\log p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\right\}},

(108)

where
(a)
leverages the Gaussianity of the approximated posterior of
\mathsf{Z}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}
,
\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{\text{prior}}
and
\nu_{\text{prior}}^{\mathcal{Z}}
represent the assumed prior mean and variance.

Intuitively, the first term in (
108
) penalizes the deviation between the (TeG-AMP approximated) posterior and the assumed prior on
\mathcal{Z}
, and the second term rewards highly likely estimates
\mathcal{U}
.

Appendix H
Tensor Simplified AMP based on CP Decomposition

Although our TeG-AMP algorithm is applicable to CP decomposition, comparing (
34
) with (
1
) we can find that the CP decomposition is a very simple special case of TR decomposition, it is possible to simplify the TeG-AMP algorithm based on the CP decomposition model. In this subsection, we present the tensor simplified AMP (TeS-AMP) algorithm. That is, estimate
\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\in\mathbb{R}
for
i=1,\ldots,d,x_{i}=1,\ldots,n_{i}
and
\ell=1,\ldots,r
from the observation of some entries of
\mathcal{V}
that is statistically coupled to
\mathcal{U}
, and is of the same dimensions as
\mathcal{U}
, with some potentially missing entries. In doing so, we consider
\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\in\mathbb{R}
for
i=1,\ldots,d,x_{i}=1,\ldots,n_{i}
and
\ell=1,\ldots,r
as realizations of independent random entries
\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)
with known separable pdfs (or pmfs in the case of discrete models)

\displaystyle p_{\mathsf{a}}(\mathbf{a})=\prod_{\ell=1}^{r}\prod_{i=1}^{d}p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\right)=\prod_{\ell=1}^{r}\prod_{i=1}^{d}\prod_{x_{i}=1}^{n_{i}}p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\right)

(109)

and we similarly assume that the likelihood function of
\mathcal{V}
is separable and known, i.e.,

\displaystyle p_{\mathsf{V}|\mathsf{U}}(\mathcal{V}|\mathcal{U})=\prod_{x_{1}=1}^{n_{1}}\ldots\prod_{x_{d}=1}^{n_{d}}p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u_{\mathbf{x}}\right).

(110)

For the statistical model (
109
)-(
110
), the posterior distribution is:

\displaystyle~p_{\mathsf{a}|\mathsf{V}}(\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{V})=p_{\left\{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\right\}_{\ell,i}|\mathsf{V}}(\{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\}_{\ell,i}|\mathcal{V})

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle~p_{\mathsf{V}|\{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\}_{\ell,i}}(\mathcal{V}|\{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\}_{\ell,i})p_{\{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\}_{\ell,i}}(\{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\}_{\ell,i})/p_{\mathsf{V}}(\mathcal{V})\propto p_{\mathsf{V}|\mathsf{U}}(\mathcal{V}|\mathcal{U})p_{\{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\}_{\ell,i}}(\{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\}_{\ell,i})

(111)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[p_{\mathsf{V}|\mathsf{U}}(\mathcal{V}|\mathcal{U})\right]\left[\prod_{\ell=1}^{r}\prod_{i=1}^{d}p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[\prod_{x_{1}=1}^{n_{1}}\ldots\prod_{x_{d}=1}^{n_{d}}p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}\mathbf{a}_{1}^{\ell}\left(x_{1}\right)\mathbf{a}_{2}^{\ell}\left(x_{2}\right)\ldots\mathbf{a}_{d}^{\ell}\left(x_{d}\right)\right)\right]\times\left[\prod_{\ell=1}^{r}\prod_{i=1}^{d}\prod_{x_{i}=1}^{n_{i}}p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\right)\right],

(112)

where
(a)
follows from Bayes’ rule.

In this section, we present TeS-AMP to compute the MMSE estimates of
\mathbf{a}=\{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\}_{\ell,i}
, i.e., the means of the marginal posteriors
p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)|\mathsf{V}}(\cdot|\mathcal{V})
, for all of the triplets
\{\ell,i,x_{i}\}
.

H.1
TeS-AMP Loopy Belief Propagation and SPA

Similar with TeG-AMP, the TeS-AMP incorporates approximations to the SPA on the factor-graph in Figure
6
. The messages that will be used in TeS-AMP are specified in Table II.

(a)

Figure 6:
An illustration of the factor-graph for tensor inference problem based on CP decomposition.

Table 2:
TeS-AMP SPA message definitions at iteration
t

{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\rightarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\cdot)

SPA message from node
\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})
to node
p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}

{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\cdot)

SPA message from node
p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}
to node
\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})

{{p}}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\cdot)

SPA-approximated log posterior pdf of message
\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})

Similar to TeG-AMP, by applying the SPA to the factor-graph of the problem, we derive the following update rules for the messages in Table II.

\displaystyle~~{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\rightarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\left(t,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\log\left(\int_{\left\{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell^{\prime}}(x_{i^{\prime}})\right\}_{i^{\prime},\ell^{\prime}}\backslash\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)}p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}\mathbf{a}_{1}^{\ell}(x_{1})\mathbf{a}_{2}^{\ell}(x_{2})\ldots\mathbf{a}_{d}^{\ell}(x_{d})\right)\right.

\displaystyle~\left.\times\prod_{\left(\ell^{\prime},i^{\prime}\right)\neq(\ell,i)}\exp\left\{{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[\ell^{\prime},i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}]}\left(t,\mathbf{a}_{i^{\prime}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(x_{i^{\prime}})\right)\right\}\right)+\text{const},

(113)

and

\displaystyle{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\left(t+1,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)

\displaystyle=\log p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)+\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}}{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\prime}\rightarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}))+\text{const}.

(114)

In the following, we indicate the mean and variance of the pdf

\frac{1}{C}\exp{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\cdot)
by
\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)
and
\nu_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)
, respectively. For the log-posterior, the SPA implies
(
Kschischang, Frey, and Loeliger 2001
)
:

\displaystyle{{p}}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\left(t+1,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)

\displaystyle=\left[\log p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)\right]+\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\prime}\rightarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}))+\text{const},

(115)

and we indicate the mean and variance of
\frac{1}{C}\exp{{p}}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\cdot)
by
\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell_{i}}(t)
and
\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)
, respectively. Next, we use AMP approximations for the SPA updates (
113
)-(
115
).

H.2
Approximated Factor-to-Variable Messages

We start by approximating the message
{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\rightarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}))
. Defining the random variable
\tilde{\mathsf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,\mathbf{x}}\triangleq{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})-\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)
with zero mean where
{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\backsim\frac{1}{C}\exp({{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\cdot))
, we can write

\displaystyle\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}\mathsf{a}_{1}^{\ell^{\prime}}(x_{1})\mathsf{a}_{2}^{\ell^{\prime}}(x_{2})\cdots\mathsf{a}_{d}^{\ell^{\prime}}(x_{d})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell^{\prime}=1\\
\ell^{\prime}\neq\ell\end{subarray}}^{r}\left(\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)+\tilde{\mathsf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}\right)\ldots\left(\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)+\tilde{\mathsf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle~~~+{\left(\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)+\tilde{\mathsf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,\mathbf{x}}\right)}\cdots{\left(\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)+\tilde{\mathsf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}\right)}{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}){\left(\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)+\tilde{\mathsf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}\right)}\cdots{\left(\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)+\tilde{\mathsf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,\mathbf{x}}\right)}.

(116)

Define
\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
and
\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)
as below that will be used in our derivations.

\displaystyle\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)},

(117)

\displaystyle\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell^{\prime}=1\\
\ell^{\prime}\neq\ell\end{subarray}}^{r}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)},

(118)

after which it is possible to see that

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})={\mathbf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)+\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)+\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\left[\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)-\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t)\right]\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t),

(119)

where
(a)
follows from similar approximations used in (
58
). Since we assumed that data size is large and
{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\cdot)
and
{{p}}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\cdot)
differ by only one term (which vanishes relative to the others in the large-system limit).

Also, define
{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
and
{\nu}^{q,\ell}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
as below that will be used in our derivations

\displaystyle{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\left(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\right),

(120)

\displaystyle{\nu}^{q,\ell}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell^{\prime}=1\\
\ell^{\prime}\neq\ell\end{subarray}}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\left(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\right),

(121)

after which it is possible to see that

\displaystyle\text{Var}\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})={\mathbf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~{\nu}^{q,\ell}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]{\mathbf{a}_{i}(x_{i})}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~{\nu}^{q,\ell}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\left[{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}^{2}-{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\right],

(122)

where
(a)
follows from similar approximations used in (
61
).

With this conditional-Gaussian approximation, (
113
) becomes

\displaystyle~{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\rightarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\log\int_{u_{\mathbf{x}}}p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\times\mathcal{N}\left(\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}};\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})={\mathbf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right\},\operatorname{Var}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})={\mathbf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right\}\right)+\text{const}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~H_{\mathbf{x}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})={\mathbf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right\},\operatorname{Var}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})={\mathbf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right\};v_{\mathbf{x}}\right)+\text{const},

(123)

in terms of the function

\displaystyle H_{\mathbf{x}}\left(\hat{q},\nu^{q};v\right)\triangleq\log\int_{u}p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}(v|u)\mathcal{N}\left(u;\hat{q},\nu^{q}\right).

(124)

Rewriting (
123
) using a Taylor series expansion in
{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}
about the point
{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t)}
, using (
119
) and (
122
) we obtain

\displaystyle~{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\rightarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}))

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle~\text{const}+H_{\mathbf{x}}(\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})

\displaystyle+\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\left[\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)-\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t)\right]\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\right]\times H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}(\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\left[\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)-\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t)\right]\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\right]^{2}\times H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime\prime}(\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})

\displaystyle+\dot{H}_{\mathbf{x}}(\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\left[{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}^{2}-{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\right]^{2}\left[\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)^{2}-2\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\right](-\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t))

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[{\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)}\right]{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg),

(125)

where (a) follows from similar approximations used in (
64
) and below definitions

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}(\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}})

(126)

\displaystyle\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq-H_{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime\prime}(\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);v_{\mathbf{x}}).

(127)

Similarly, given (
126
)-(
127
) and (
124
) the followings hold

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)}\left(\hat{u}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\right),

(128)

\displaystyle{\nu}_{q,\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)}\left(1-\frac{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)}\right),

(129)

for the conditional mean and variance

\displaystyle\hat{u}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{q}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)\right\},

(130)

\displaystyle\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)

\displaystyle\triangleq\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{q}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)\right\},

(131)

computed according to the conditional pdf

\displaystyle p_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{q}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(u|\hat{q};\nu^{q}\right)\triangleq\frac{p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\mathcal{N}(u_{\mathbf{x}};\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t))}{\int_{u^{\prime}}p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u^{\prime}\right)\mathcal{N}(u^{\prime};\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t))}.

(132)

The same, (
132
) is TeS-AMP’s the
t
-th iteration approximation to the true marginal posterior
p_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{V}}(\cdot|\mathcal{V})
. We note that (
132
) can also be interpreted as the posterior pdf for
\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}
given the likelihood
p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|\cdot\right)
from (
110
) and the prior
\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}\sim\mathcal{N}(\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t))
that is implicitly assumed by the
t
-th iteration TeS-AMP.

H.3
Approximated Variable-to-Factor Messages

We now turn to approximating the messages flowing from the variable nodes to the factor nodes. Starting with (
114
) and plugging in (
125
) we obtain

\displaystyle~{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t+1,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\log p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)+\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}}{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\prime}\rightarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}))+\text{const}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\log p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)+\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}}\Bigg\{\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\ldots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)\right]^{2}\left[\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)^{2}-2\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\right](-\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t))

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left[{\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)}\right]{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\log p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)-\frac{1}{2\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}}\left({\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}-\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\text{const}+\log\left(p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}))\mathcal{N}\left({\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})};\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\right)\right),

(133)

where
(a)
follows from (
125
),
(b)
follows from similar derivations used in (
74
) and the following definitions:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}}\Bigg\{\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle~~~~~-\left[{\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\},

(134)

and

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}=

\displaystyle~\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}}\Bigg\{\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1+\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}}\left[{\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\times

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}.

(135)

The mean and variance of the pdf associated with the
{{p}}_{\mathbf{x}\leftarrow[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t+1,\cdot)
approximation in (
133
) are

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t+1)\triangleq\underbrace{\frac{1}{C}\int_{x}x~p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}(x)\mathcal{N}\left(x;\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\right)}_{\triangleq~g_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]})},

(136)

and

\displaystyle\nu_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t+1)\triangleq\underbrace{\frac{1}{C}\int_{x}|x-\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t+1)|^{2}~p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}(x)\mathcal{N}\left(x;\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\right)}_{\triangleq~\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}g_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]})},

(137)

where here
C=\int_{x}p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}(x)\mathcal{N}\left(x;\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\right)
and
g_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}^{\prime}
denotes the derivative of
g_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}
with respect to the first argument. The fact that (
136
) and (
137
) are related through a derivative was shown in
(
Rangan 2011
)
.

To derive approximations of
\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}
and
\nu_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}
that avoid the dependence on the destination node
u_{\mathbf{x}}
, we similarly introduce
{\mathbf{x}}
-invariant versions of
\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}
and
{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}}
as follows

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}}\triangleq

\displaystyle~\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle~-\left[{\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\},

(138)

and

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}=

\displaystyle~\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1+\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i},~\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\neq\mathbf{x}}\left[{\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-{\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\right]\times

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}.

(139)

Remark H.1
.

Comparing (
138
)-(
139
) with (
134
)-(
135
) suggest that
{\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}}-{\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}}
is relatively negligible and hence each
\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}
can be approximated by the term

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}-\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{z}}^{\ell}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t),

(140)

so that (
136
) implies the following approximation.

Similarly with (
83
), by applying Remark
H.1
and Taylor series expansion in the first argument about the point
\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}
we have

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t+1)=g_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}\left(\hat{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{\mathbf{x},[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\right)

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle~\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t+1)-\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t+1)\Big[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,x_{i-1}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,x_{i+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big],

(141)

where

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t+1)\triangleq g_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}\left(\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\right),

(142)

and

\displaystyle\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t+1)\triangleq\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}g_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}^{\prime}\left(\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\right),

(143)

which match (
136
)-(
137
) without the
\mathbf{x}
-dependence.

Remark H.2
.

Likewise, taking Taylor series expansions of
g_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}^{\prime}
in (
137
) about the point
\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}
in the first argument and about the point
\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}
in the second argument and then comparing the result with (
143
) confirms that
\nu_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)-\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)
is negligible.

H.4
Completing the Loop of TeS-AMP Algorithm

The penultimate step in the derivation of TeS-AMP algorithm is to approximate some earlier steps that use
\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell}(t)
in place of
\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t)
. Similarly, we plug (
142
) into (
117
) to obtain

\displaystyle~~~~~~~\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)=\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}\underbrace{\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}}_{\bar{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~~~~\Big[-\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t-1)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t-1)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\approx}\bar{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~~~~\Big[-\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}\bar{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\bar{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),

(144)

where
(a)
follows from (
142
);
(b)
follows noting that we assumed data size is large and equations on two side of this differ by only one term (which vanishes relative to the others in the large-system limit) and we used
\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}(t)
in place of
\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}(t-1)
; and
(c)
follows from the following definition:

\displaystyle\bar{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big(\prod_{i\in A}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Big)^{2}\Big(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\prod_{i^{\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}(t)\Big)^{d-|A|-1}\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}.

(145)

Note that different with
\bar{\nu}^{p}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
in TeG-AMP, which uses the approximation in Remark
E.1
, the
\bar{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
in the last step in (
144
) is an exact result.

Next we plug (
142
) and
\nu_{i,\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\approx\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)
into (
120
), which yields

\displaystyle{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\approx

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\left(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell=1\end{subarray}}^{r}\prod_{i\in\{1,...,d\}}\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\Bigg[\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)-

\displaystyle~\Big[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]^{2}\Bigg),

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell=1\end{subarray}}^{r}\prod_{i\in\{1,...,d\}}\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)+\bar{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

(146)

where
(a)
follows from (
142
) and that we used
\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}(t)
in place of
\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}(t-1)
, and
(b)
follows from Remark
H.3
.

Remark H.3
.

We observed that the following approximation holds

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell^{\prime}=1\end{subarray}}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\Bigg[\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)-

\displaystyle~~~\Big[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{1,x_{1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{d,x_{d}}(t)\Big]\Bigg\},

(147)

where the approximation follows from similar derivations and approximations used in Remark
E.2
.

Similarly we plug (
142
) into (
139
) to obtain

\displaystyle~\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\Bigg[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle~+\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{1-\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\Bigg[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle~+\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\left\{1-\frac{\sum\limits_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\\
\setminus\{i\},A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]}{\sum\limits_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\Bigg\}}\right\},

(148)

where
(a)
follows from neglecting the below term with the same arguments as before.

\displaystyle\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle-\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\Bigg[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\Bigg\{\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~-\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)^{2}\Bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)\right)^{2}\left(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t-1)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)\right)^{|A|-1}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\Bigg\{\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg[\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle{\approx}

\displaystyle~\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(t)\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)^{2}\Bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\end{subarray}}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)\right)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle~\left(1-\left(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t-1)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)\right)^{|A|-1}\right)\Bigg\},

(149)

where
(a)
follows Remark (
H.4
) and we used
\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)
in replace of
\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t-1)
. We observed that the term (
149
) is small. If under specific applications this terms was not negligible, it can simply be included back in the algorithm. And for
(b)
, we had the approximation:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}}\approx\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t)\right]^{2}\nu_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{s}(t)\Bigg\},

(150)

by neglecting
{\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)}^{2}-\nu^{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)
terms in (
139
) as explained in Appendix B in
(
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014a
)
.

Remark H.4
.

The following holds:

\displaystyle~\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\left[\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\right]\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\sum_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}:x^{\prime}_{i}=x_{i}}\Bigg\{\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg[{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t-1)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)\right)^{2}\left(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t-1)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)\right)^{|A|-1}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\Bigg]\Bigg\},

(151)

where
(a)
follows from

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i-1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i+1,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t-1)\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)

\displaystyle~~~\Big[-\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i^{\prime\prime}-1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}^{\prime}}(t-1)\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i^{\prime\prime}+1,x_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{\prime}}(t-1)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}(t-1)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\approx}

\displaystyle~{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,x_{1}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i-1,x_{i-1}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i+1,x_{i+1}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,x_{d}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)}-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t-1)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\setminus\{i\}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus(\{i\}\cup A)\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)\right)^{2}\left(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}(t-1)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\end{subarray}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\ell}(t)\right)^{|A|-1}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime\prime}\in A\end{subarray}}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t),

(152)

where
(a)
follows from (
142
) and in
(b)
we used
\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}(t)
in place of
\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{\prime}}(t-1)
.

H.5
Approximated Posteriors

The final step in the TeS-AMP derivation is also to approximate the SPA posterior log-pdfs in (
115
). Plugging (
125
) into those expressions, we get

\displaystyle{{p}}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}(t+1,\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i}))\approx\text{const}+\log\left(p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\right)\mathcal{N}\left({\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})};\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}\right)\right),

(153)

using steps similar to (
133
). The associated pdfs are

\displaystyle p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)|\mathsf{r}_{\left[\ell,i,x_{i}\right]}}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)|\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]};\nu_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}^{r}\right)\triangleq\frac{p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)\right)\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right);\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}^{r}\right)}{\int_{a^{\prime}}p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)}\left(\mathbf{a}^{\prime}\right)\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{a}^{\prime};\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}^{r}\right)}

(154)

which are the
t
-th iteration approximations to the true marginal posteriors
p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)|\mathsf{V}}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)|\mathcal{V}\right)
.

Note also that
\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t+1)
and
\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t+1)
from (
142
)-(
143
) are the mean and variance, respectively, of the posterior pdf in (
154
), and (
154
) is interpreted as the posterior pdf of
{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})
given the observation
\mathsf{r}_{\left[\ell,i,x_{i}\right]}=\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}
under the prior model
{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})\sim p_{{\mathsf{a}}_{i}^{\ell}(x_{i})}
and the likelihood model

\displaystyle p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)|\mathsf{r}_{\left[\ell,i,x_{i}\right]}}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)|\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]};\nu_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}^{r}\right)=\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right);\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]},\nu_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}^{r}\right)

(155)

implicitly assumed by the
t
-th iteration TeS-AMP. To this end, we complete the derivation of TeS-AMP.

The TeS-AMP algorithm derived is summarized in Algorithm
2
, where we have also written the algorithm in a general form that allows the use of complex-valued quantities. Comparing with TeG-AMP in Algorithm 1, the computational load of TeS-AMP is reduced, making it more suitable for real-time implementation when a tensor is low CP-rank.

Algorithm 2

Tensor Simplified Approximate Message Passing (TeS-AMP) Algorithm.

Initialization
:
p_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{q}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(u|\hat{q};\nu^{q}\right)=\frac{p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u\right)\mathcal{N}(u;\hat{q}(t),\nu^{q}(t))}{\int_{u^{\prime}}p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u^{\prime}\right)\mathcal{N}(u^{\prime};\hat{q}(t),\nu^{q}(t))}
;

p_{\mathsf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)|\mathsf{r}_{\left[\ell,i,x_{i}\right]}}\left(a|\hat{r};\nu^{r}\right)=\frac{p\left(a\right)\mathcal{N}\left(a;\hat{r},\nu^{r}\right)}{\int_{a^{\prime}}p\left(a^{\prime}\right)\mathcal{N}\left(a^{\prime};\hat{r},\nu^{r}\right)}
.
\forall i,x_{i},~\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(0)=0
;
\forall i,\ell,x_{i}
,
choose

\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(1)
,
\nu^{\mathbf{a},\ell}_{i,x_{i}}(1)
.

1:
for

t=1,\cdots,T_{\text{max}}

do

2:
\bar{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\leftarrow\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}\cdots{\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)}
[according to (
144
)]

3: Update
\bar{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
[according to (
145
)]

4: Update
\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
[according to (
144
)]

5: Update
\nu^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)
[according to (
146
)]

6:
\nu_{q,\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)\leftarrow\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{q}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)\right\}
[according to (
131
)]

7:
\hat{u}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)\leftarrow\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{q}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t);\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)\right\}
[according to (
130
)]

8:
\hat{s}_{q,\mathbf{x}}(t)\leftarrow\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)}\left(\hat{u}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\right)
[according to (
128
)]

9:
{\nu}_{q,\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)\leftarrow\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)}\left(1-\frac{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)}\right)
[according to (
129
)]

10: Update
\nu^{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}
[according to (
150
)]

11: Update
\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}
[according to (
148
)]

12:
\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t+1)\leftarrow\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathsf{a}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}|\mathsf{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}=\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]};\nu_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}^{r}\right\}
[according to (
154
)]

13:
\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t+1)\leftarrow\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathsf{a}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}|\mathsf{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}=\hat{r}_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]};\nu_{[\ell,i,x_{i}]}^{r}\right\}
[according to (
154
)]

14:
if

\sum_{\mathbf{x}}|\bar{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\bar{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)|^{2}\leq\tau_{\text{threshold}}\sum_{\mathbf{x}}|\bar{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)|^{2}

then

15:
stop

16:
end for

H.6
Damping & Adaptive Damping for TeS-AMP

Similarly, to adopt a damping strategy, we can also use a damping factor
\beta(t)\in(0,1]
at the
t
-th iteration to slow the evolution of certain variables in TeS-AMP. To do this, steps 3, 5, 8, and 9 in Algorithm
2
are replaced with

\displaystyle\bar{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle=(1-\beta(t))\bar{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)+\beta(t)\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subsetneqq\{1,...,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\Big(\prod_{i\in A}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Big)^{2}\times

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Big(-\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1)\prod_{i^{\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}}\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell^{\prime}}_{i^{\prime},x_{i^{\prime}}}(t)\Big)^{d-|A|-1}\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,...,d\}\setminus A}\nu_{i^{\prime\prime},x_{i^{\prime\prime}}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\Bigg\},

(156)

\displaystyle\nu^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta(t)\left(\bar{\nu}^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell=1\end{subarray}}^{r}\prod_{i\in\{1,...,d\}}\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\right)+(1-\beta(t))\nu^{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1),

(157)

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta(t)\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)}\left(\hat{u}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)-\hat{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t)\right)+(1-\beta(t))\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}(t-1),

(158)

\displaystyle{\nu}_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta(t)\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)}\left(1-\frac{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{u}(t)}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{q}(t)}\right)+(1-\beta(t)){\nu}_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}(t-1),

(159)

and the following are inserted between step 9 and step 10 in Algorithm
2
:

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta(t){\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t)+(1-\beta(t)){\bar{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t-1).

(160)

The newly defined state variables
{\bar{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t)
are then used in place of
{\hat{\mathbf{a}}^{\ell}_{i,x_{i}}}(t)
in step 13 in Algorithm
2
.

In addition, the adaptive damping strategy can be easily implemented by following (
102
)-(
105
) in Appendix
G
with
{\mathsf{Z}}
and
\mathcal{Z}
respectively replaced by
{\mathsf{A}}
and
\mathcal{A}
, where we also approximate the cost (
104
) by using, in place of
\mathcal{U}
, an independent Gaussian tensor whose component means and variances are matched to those of
\mathcal{U}
. Taking the joint TeS-AMP posterior approximation
b_{\mathsf{A}}(t)
to be the product of the marginals from (
132
), the resulting component means and variances are

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{A}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{A}}(t)}\left\{\mathsf{a}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell}\right\}\cdots\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{A}}(t)}\left\{\mathsf{a}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell}\right\}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{1,x_{1}}^{\ell}(t)\cdots\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{d,x_{d}}^{\ell}(t)=\bar{q}_{\mathbf{x}}(t),

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}_{b_{\mathsf{A}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}A\subset\{1,\ldots,d\}\\
A\neq\emptyset\end{subarray}}\left(\prod_{i^{\prime}\in A}\nu_{i^{\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell^{\prime}}(t)\prod_{i^{\prime\prime}\in\{1,\ldots,d\}\backslash A}\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{x}}^{\ell^{\prime}}(t)^{2}\right).

(161)

The approximate
t
-th iteration cost becomes

\displaystyle\hat{J}(t)=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq d\\
1\leq\ell\leq r\\
1\leq x_{i}\leq n_{i}\end{subarray}}D\left(p_{\mathsf{a}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}|r_{\left[\ell,i,x_{i}\right]}}\left(\cdot|\hat{r}_{\left[\ell,i,x_{i}\right]}(t);\nu_{\left[\ell,i,x_{i}\right]}^{r}(t)\right)\|p_{\mathsf{a}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}}(\cdot)\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{A}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\};\mathrm{Var}_{b_{\mathsf{A}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\}\right)}{\left\{\log p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\right\}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq d\\
1\leq\ell\leq r\\
1\leq x_{i}\leq n_{i}\end{subarray}}\int_{\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right)}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right);\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t),\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\right)\log\frac{\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right);\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}(t),\nu_{i,x_{i}}^{\mathbf{a},\ell}(t)\right)}{\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\ell}\left(x_{i}\right);\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{\text{prior}},\nu_{\text{prior}}^{\mathbf{a}}\right)}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbb{E}_{b_{\mathsf{A}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\};\mathrm{Var}_{b_{\mathsf{A}}(t)}\left\{u_{\mathbf{x}}\right\}\right)}{\left\{\log p_{\mathsf{v}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathsf{u}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(v_{\mathbf{x}}|u_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\right\}},

(162)

where
(a)
leverages the Gaussianity of the approximated posterior of
\mathsf{a}_{i,x_{i}}^{\ell}
,
\hat{\mathbf{a}}_{\text{prior}}
and
\nu_{\text{prior}}^{\mathbf{a}}
represent the assumed prior mean and variance. Intuitively, the first term in (
162
) penalizes the deviation between the (TeS-AMP approximated) posterior and the assumed prior on
\mathcal{A}
, and the second term rewards highly likely estimates
\mathcal{U}
.

Appendix I
Additional Experimental Results

I.1
Experimental Setup

We consider a particular observation model wherein the elements of
\mathcal{U}
are potentially AWGN-corrupted at a subset of indices
\Omega
and unobserved at the remaining indices, which validates the performance of the proposed algorithm applied to tensor completion application in (
2
). For the performance of the proposed algorithm applied to tensor decomposition, we set
\Omega
to all ones to satisfy the model in (
2
).

At the observed entries
\mathbf{x}\in\Omega
, the quantities
\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}
and
\nu^{s}_{\mathbf{x}}
are respectively calculated using the AWGN expressions (
17
) and (
18
), while at the missing entries
\mathbf{x}\notin\Omega
, where
v_{\mathbf{x}}
in invariant to the value of
u_{\mathbf{x}}
, we will have the mean
\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathrm{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathrm{p}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}};\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}\right\}=\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}

and the variance
\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathrm{u}_{\mathbf{x}}|\mathrm{p}_{\mathbf{x}}=\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}};\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}\right\}=\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}
,
so that
\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}=0
and
\nu^{s}_{\mathbf{x}}=0
. This shows that
\nu^{s}
can be interpreted as inverse residual variances and
\hat{s}
as
\nu^{s}
-scaled residuals. In summary, the possibly incomplete AWGN model yields

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{\mathbf{x}}=

\displaystyle~\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}{\frac{v_{\mathbf{x}}-\hat{p}_{\mathbf{x}}}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}+\nu^{w}}}&{{\mathbf{x}}\in\Omega,}\\
{0}&{{\mathbf{x}}\notin\Omega,}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{s}=

\displaystyle~\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}{\frac{1}{\nu_{\mathbf{x}}^{p}+\nu^{w}}}&{{\mathbf{x}}\in\Omega,}\\
{0}&{{\mathbf{x}}\notin\Omega.}\end{array}\right.

Similar to the approaches in
(
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014a
;
Parker, Schniter, and
Cevher 2014b
;
Vila and Schniter 2013
)
we select the variance

\displaystyle\nu^{w}=\frac{\left\|P_{\Omega}(\mathcal{V})\right\|_{F}^{2}}{(\text{SNR}+1)|\Omega|},

(167)

where SNR is an initial estimate of the signal-to-noise ratio that can be set at
100
.

Regarding the termination of the algorithm, we terminate the algorithm when it converges or diverges. The algorithm has been converged if the generated tensor at the end of an iteration is very close to that in the previous iterations (when the Frobenius norm of their difference divided by the Frobenius norm of the previous one is less than
0.001
). And the algorithm has diverged if the Frobenius norm of the generated tensor at the end of an iteration divided by the Frobenius norm of the generated tensor at the end of the first iteration is more than
10^{6}
.

Assume that we want to generate a tensor of size
n_{1}\times\cdots\times n_{d}
with TR-rank
r_{1},\cdots,r_{d}
with random components. We consider the TR decomposition and choose each entry of
\mathcal{Z}_{i}^{\ell_{i},\ell_{i+1}}
independently and according to standard Gaussian distribution
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
, for
1\leq i\leq d,~1\leq\ell_{i}\leq r_{i}
. Then, we generate the tensor according to (
1
